平稳点的样本复杂度为 O(Δ_1 L / ε^2 · (σ/ε)^{p/(p-1)})。当 p=2 时退化到更熟悉的有界方差型行为；当 p<2 时，重尾噪声会通过 (σ/ε)^{p/(p-1)} 体现出更重的代价，但这个代价是在原生几何里计算的。\n\n第二，对 Muon 关心的 m×n 矩阵情形，论文给出核范数平稳性的复杂度上界，主导维度因子是 min{m,n}。这里作者强调的是：如果用欧式方法并要求同样强的 Schatten-1 stationarity，维度代价会更差，而且还会带额外的 p 相关恶化；Muon 的上界在一阶方法里已接近可达到极限。\n\n第三，论文给出 Schatten-1 stationarity 的 lower bound，说明不存在 dimension-free guarantee。也就是说，Muon 结果里残留的 min{m,n} 不是分析松弛造成的，而是问题本身决定的。这一点很重要，因为它把“Muon 还有维度因子，所以理论不够好”的直觉纠正过来：对核范数平稳性而言，完全去维度化并不现实。\n\n如果全文里包含数值实验，它们更可能是用 toy problem 或合成 heavy-tailed noise 验证理论斜率，而不是在 GPT 级训练上做系统 benchmark。阅读时不必期待参数量、token 数、下游任务分数这类预训练指标；真正该看的，是不同几何下复杂度项如何比较，以及 lower bound 与 upper bound 的匹配程度。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction、main theorem 和 Muon 专门结果，再决定是否深入证明。预计 30 到 45 reduction；二是为什么核范数不 uniformly smooth，却仍能做出专门分析。若你做优化器研究，值得深挖 proof 中控制噪声项的工具，因为这部分可能能迁移到其他非欧式或矩阵结构优化器。若你做预训练工程，重点不是逐行看证明，而是建立一个判断框架：当梯度噪声重尾、参数天然是矩阵、目标平稳性更接近谱/核范数几何时，Muon training, while existing theory does not really explain their advantage? Prior analyses usually assume bounded-variance noise and then reduce the stochastic term back to Euclidean geometry through norm equivalence. That move introduces dimension-dependent penalties, especially for Muon, where the natural dual stationarity measure is the nuclear norm rather than the Frobenius norm. This work studies the more realistic heavy-tailed regime, assuming only bounded p-th central moments of stochastic gradients in the native dual geometry, with p in (1,2]. The goal is to show that non-Euclidean normalized steepest-descent methods can provably outperform Euclidean methods under stronger stationarity measures, and that Muon’s empirical success is tied to the geometry it uses rather than being an accidental implementation detail.","importance":"$1d","technical_approach":"$1e","experiments":"$1f","reading_guide":"$20","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"Muon优化器的首个重尾非凸场景理论证明，解释其训练Transformer的优势，做优化器或训练recipe的研究员重点看§3-§4的定理推导","en":"First theoretical justification of Muon's edge in heavy-tailed non-convex Transformer training; optimizer/training recipe researchers should focus on theorems in §3-§4"},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Florian Hübler","Thomas Pethick","Suvrit Sra"],"notable_authors":["Suvrit Sra"],"tags":["Muon optimizer","non-Euclidean optimization","heavy-tailed gradients","Transformer training","sample complexity"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14560","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.14560","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.14187","title":"Zeta: Dual Whitening for Matrix Optimization via Coordinate-Adaptive Preconditioning","abstract":"Large-scale neural network training increasingly relies on matrix-aware optimizers that exploit the structure of weight parameters beyond element-wise adaptation. strong coordinate-wise scale imbalance. Poorly conditioned inputs make Newton–Schulz less accurate and can distort the matrix update direction. Zeta addresses this mismatch by normalizing coordinate scales before applying spectral whitening.","importance":"The paper is relevant to pretraining optimizer design. Its useful contribution is not just another optimizer variant, but a clearer decomposition of two adaptation mechanisms: element-wise statistical stabilization and matrix-level spectral control. Instead of treating Adam-like coordinate adaptation and Muon-like orthogonalization as competing choices, Zeta makes them sequential. This is a practical observation for large model training, where optimizer changes often fail because an operation that is sound geometrically is applied to badly scaled training signals.","technical_approach":"Zeta applies a two-stage whitening pipeline. The first stage performs coordinate whitening by dividing the momentum entries by running second-moment estimates, giving an approximate diagonal preconditioner. This reduces coordinate-level energy imbalance. The second stage performs spectral whitening with Newton–Schulz iteration, equalizing singular values and producing an orthogonalized matrix update. The ordering is the main design choice: spectral whitening is sensitive to conditioning, so it should operate on a statistically stabilized matrix rather than raw momentum. The theory supports this view in three steps: coordinate whitening behaves like diagonal preconditioning under standard assumptions; the preconditioned matrix has a better condition number, reducing Newton–Schulz orthogonalization error; RMS scaling preserves the spectral direction rather than replacing the matrix geometry with a purely element-wise update.","experiments":"The paper first validates the failure mode with a chi-square uniformity test on row energies of representative Transformer weight matrices. Raw momentum matrices show consistently small p-values across layers, indicating strong intra-matrix scale imbalance. After coordinate whitening, the p-values move toward 1, suggesting that the imbalance is substantially reduced. The training experiments cover language model pretraining from 0.6B to 8B parameters, mixture-of-experts models, and vision tasks. Baselines include AdamW, Muon, and AdaMuon. The reported outcome is that Zeta matches or improves convergence speed and downstream performance across these settings. The provided content does not include exact benchmark tables or numerical gains, so precise effect sizes should be checked in the experimental section.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough for the main idea. This matters for multimodal agents that use generated videos for planning, where trajectory correctness is more important than visual fidelity.","technical_approach":"Temporal Backtracking Search turns video generation into a generate–verify–restart loop. A rollout is checked by a process verifier, which identifies the first failing frame. The valid prefix before that frame is kept as a restart anchor, and the model regenerates only the suffix. The method relies on variable-K conditioning, a training setup that lets the diffusion model resume generation from clean prefixes of arbitrary temporal length while preserving motion context. Compared with root-level Best-of-N, TBS avoids throwing away correct early progress and reallocates inference budget to unresolved suffixes.","experiments":"The evaluation covers symbolic grid-worlds with exact verification, continuous navigation with learned noisy trajectory verification, and robotic manipulation with simulator-replay verification. loss，而是在模型参数中预留一类稀疏激活的 sink neurons，让源特异信息在训练动态中自然集中到对应 sink 中，共享信息仍由 backbone 学习。这个方向对基座训练有直接影响：如果数据合规、版权删除、用户数据撤回要成为训练系统的内生能力，单靠训练后编辑很难可靠；需要在预训练结构、数据 sinks。实现上主要是在 MLP activation 上做 elementwise mask，训练流程接近标准预训练。关键点是它没有为每个 source 分配一套独立参数，而是用稀疏 mask 在 sink pool 上形成组合式控制，因此 source 数量可以很大，参数量不随 source 数线性增长。训练动态解释也很重要：某个只出现在单一 source 的事实，会同时给 backbone 和该 source 的 sinks 提供梯度；但 backbone 会受到所有其他 source 的干扰更新，sink 只在较少 source 中激活，干扰更小，所以源特异事实更快被 sinks 拟合。一旦 sinks 已经解释了这部分预测需求，backbone 上的梯度压力下降，泄漏会减弱。重复出现在多个 source 中的信息则持续强化 backbone，因此删除单一 source 的 sinks 不会删除跨源共享知识。部署时 unlearning 只需关闭目标 source 对应的 sinks。","experiments":"论文做了两个主要 case study。第一个是在 Wikipedia corpus 上训练约 1B 参数模型，将约 600 万篇文章视为独立 source，测试能否删除单篇文章而不造成主题级别遗忘。结果显示，关闭目标文章的 sinks 后，模型对该文章独有事实的 recall 明显下降，同时能保留语义相关、来自其他文章的知识；post-hoc unlearning 方法则更容易在删除目标知识的同时破坏相邻知识。第二个是 Harry Potter case study，source 粒度更粗，偏主题或作品集合。NULLs 可以在部署时即时移除对应 source，并且对 adversarial relearning 更稳；论文指出，常规 gradient unlearning 在少于 10 个 gradient steps 内就可被反向恢复，而 NULLs 的删除更难被这种攻击逆转。通用能力方面，NULLs 在自然语言 downstream benchmarks 上与标准 Transformer 接近，说明稀疏 sink 机制没有明显牺牲一般性能。需要进一步关注的实验细节包括 sink pool 占比、每个 source mask 的稀疏度、不同 source 粒度下的容量压力、mask collision 对删除精度的影响，以及关闭 sinks 后 perplexity 与事实 recall 的变化是否一致。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Introduction、方法部分和两个 case study 的主实验。Introduction 中关于 post-hoc unlearning 与 per-source module 的对比写得很清楚，能帮助定位 NULLs 解决的不是单一算法问题，而是训练结构问题。方法部分要重点看 source mask 如何定义、MLP activation 如何被分成 backbone 与 sinks、训练时哪些参数会被激活、删除时具体关闭什么。实验部分优先看 Wikipedia 单文章删除：这是最接近真实大规模 provenance 场景的设置。再看 Harry Potter 的 adversarial relearning，对理解“删除是否只是表面抑制”很有帮助。值得深挖的点有三个：一是源特异信息是否真的只留在 sinks，还是仍有 backbone leakage；二是当 source 数达到百万级且 mask 有重叠时，组合式 sink 控制的容量边界在哪里；三是如果数据源定义在训练后才变化，NULLs 不能直接处理，这要求预训练阶段就有稳定的 provenance 设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses source-level removability in pretrained LLMs. Standard training entangles the influence of documents, publishers, or other data sources in shared weights, so later removing one source often also damages related knowledge that should remain.","importance":"This is a must-read pretraining architecture paper because it turns unlearning from a post-hoc repair problem into a training-time design problem. NULLs does not mainly propose another unlearning loss. It creates a parameter interface in which source-specific information is encouraged to concentrate in sparse sink neurons, while shared information remains in the backbone. This matters for foundation-model training systems where copyright removal, user-data withdrawal, provenance control, and attribution need to be handled at data-source granularity. It also gives a more concrete route to data attribution: if a source’s contribution is localized, its influence can be measured and disabled directly.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"$22","reading_guide":"Read the introduction, method section, and both case studies carefully. The introduction frames the central tradeoff well: post-hoc unlearning preserves joint training but leaves source influence entangled, while per-source modules make deletion easy but weaken joint learning. The method section is the core: inspect how source masks are assigned, how MLP activations are split into backbone and sinks, which neurons are active during training, and what exactly is disabled at inference. In the experiments, prioritize the Wikipedia single-article deletion setup because it is the closest to large-scale provenance control. Then read the Harry Potter adversarial relearning experiment to assess whether deletion is more than temporary output suppression. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor diffusion language model 在真实推理时，到底按什么顺序“提交”token。论文研究的不是训练算法，而是一个已发布 checkpoint 的解码行为：google/diffusiongemma-26B-A4B-it，一个基于 Gemma 4 的 masked discrete diffusion MoE 模型，25.2B 总参数、3.8B 激活参数、8-of-128 expert routing。作者指出，DLM 常被描述成并行、非自回归解码器，但“架构允许并行”和“模型实际并行提交”不是一回事。真实提交顺序由模型、sampler、停止准则共同决定，需要直接测量。","importance":"对预训练和新型语言建模研究的价值在于，它把 diffusion LM 的一个核心承诺拆成了可观测量。DLM 的低延迟潜力通常来自多位置并行提交；如果实际 checkpoint 在许多任务上呈现较强的左到右偏置，吞吐和延迟分析就不能只看理论 denoising 步数。论文的结论也约束了后续建模判断：DiffusionGemma 既不是严格 AR，也不是完全 order-independent，而是有粒度依赖的部分左到右提交偏置。这个结果对 DLM 训练目标、sampler 设计、评测协议都有启发。尤其是它提醒研究者，不要用粗 bin 或肉眼样例把模型误读成 block-autoregressive；同一个生成过程在 token 级、bin 级、accept-call 级会呈现不同结构。","technical_approach":"方法是纯 inference instrumentation，不训练、不微调。作者 hook 了 transformers denoising loop 中的 EntropyBoundSampler.accept_canvas，在每次 accept-call 记录哪些 canvas 位置被提交，以及这些位置的 per-position entropy。这里的“提交”指 sampler 接受某些位置的当前预测并写入生成 canvas。基于这些 trace，论文计算提交顺序与左到右位置顺序之间的 tie-aware Kendall tau_b。tie-aware 很关键，因为一次 accept-call 往往会同时提交十几个到二十几个 token，同一 call 内没有可解析的先后顺序。作者还做了不同 bin size 下的顺序相关性分析，用来区分 token 级无序、粗粒度左到右、以及真正的 block-sequential。为避免伪结论，论文显式处理了 EOS padding artifact、跨 regime pooling 导致的 Simpson's paradox、commit non-monotonicity、block-size sensitivity 和 same-call ties。与常见只看输出文本的分析不同，这篇论文测的是 sampler 内部实际接受 token 的时刻，因此更接近推理系统真正执行的生成路径。","experiments":"实验覆盖 686 个 prompt、6 类 regime。强化实验设置为 5 个 seed，每个 regime 20 个 prompt，共 100 条 trace/regime，并用 prompt-clustered bootstrap 给出置信区间。主要结果是：除 JSON 外，多数 regime 在 token 级呈中等左到右偏置，prose、code、math、factual 等 regime 的 tie-aware Kendall tau_b 约为 0.43 到 0.60。随着分析 bin 变粗，左到右相关性平滑上升，例如 R1 从 bin=4 的 0.59 升到 bin=64 的 0.91，但在 16-token 附近没有跳变。这说明 16 更像分析者选择的 bin，不是模型内部固定 block。真实 token order 明显低于纯 block-sequential control，后者约 0.94 到 0.96，说明子 block 内有实际无序。每次 accept-call 平均提交约 13 到 26 个 content token，同 call tie 比例较大，因此 token 级顺序只能在 accept-call 分辨率上测量。JSON regime 很不同，token tau_b=-0.044，95% CI 为 [-0.086, -0.00]，更接近 order-independent，而不是强非顺序。置信度与正确性的关系也依赖任务：在 GSM8K 上，commit confidence 即负熵对正确性有区分度，AUROC=0.749，95% CI [0.602, 0.879]，按 entropy tertile 看 reliability 单调；在 factual recall 上 AUROC=0.471，95% CI [0.383, 0.544]，基本没有可用信号。生成通常在很少的 accept-call 内完成，远低于 48-step budget，且接受时 entropy 远低于 0.1 bound。可评分 regime 上，DiffusionGemma 的任务准确率与 AR sibling Gemma-4 26B-A4B 接近，但作者明确说明实验没有为 equivalence test 做功效设计。","reading_guide":"建议精读 §5.1 和 §6.1。§5.1 是核心，重点看 token-level tau_b、bin-size 曲线、block-sequential control、commit batch size 和 JSON regime 的对照。这里能看清为什么“部分左到右偏置”比“block-autoregressive”更准确。§5.2 建议看 commit-order 研究容易踩的坑。若时间有限，先读 Introduction 的 falsifiable claims，再读 §5.1 的图表和 §6.1 的 methodology caveats。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrow but important empirical question: when an open diffusion language model is actually run, in what order does it commit tokens? The target is google/diffusiongemma-26B-A4B-it, a masked discrete-diffusion MoE model built on Gemma 4, with 25.2B total parameters and 3.8B active parameters through 8-of-128 expert routing. The study separates architectural possibility from runtime behavior. A DLM can denoise many positions in parallel, but the checkpoint and sampler may still produce a partially ordered commitment process.","importance":"The result is useful for researchers working on non-AR language modeling because it turns a common claim about diffusion LMs into a measurable property. Parallel denoising is often used to motivate lower latency, but latency depends on how many positions are accepted per step and whether commitment is truly out of order. The main finding is not that DiffusionGemma behaves like an AR model, nor that it is fully order-independent. It shows a partial, granularity-dependent left-to-right bias. This matters for sampler design, training objective analysis, and evaluation protocols for DLMs. It also cautions against reading coarse-bin traces as evidence of clean block-autoregressive generation.","technical_approach":"The work is inference-only. The authors do not train or fine-tune the model. They instrument the denoising loop by hooking EntropyBoundSampler.accept_canvas and record, for each accept-call, the canvas positions that become committed and the entropy at those positions. Commitment order is then measured with tie-aware Kendall tau_b between position order and accept order. The tie-aware treatment is necessary because a single accept-call often commits many positions, and positions committed in the same call have no observable internal order. The paper also studies bin-size dependence, compares the observed order against a pure block-sequential control, and analyzes whether negative entropy at commit time predicts correctness. A notable part of the methodology is its handling of artifacts: EOS padding, cross-regime pooling, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and same-call ties.","experiments":"$23","reading_guide":"Read Section 5.1 closely. CoT 推理的串行开销问题：标准自回归推理每生成一个思维 token 都要做一次前向计算，而直接转向连续 latent reasoning 又容易丢掉 token 级监督，训练不稳定。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在建模接口，而不是完整的基座预训练 recipe。SuperThoughts 提供了一个折中点：不完全放弃离散 CoT，也不让模型在无约束 latent 重排。每一步不再消费单个 CoT token，而是由 Compressor 将两个 token embedding 压成一个 H 维向量。Compressor 有两种实现：简单线性投影，把两个 embedding 拼接后映射回 hidden size；或用一个小 Transformer block 处理 token pair，再取第二个位置的 hidden state。随后主干模型预测下一对中的第一个 token，轻量 MTP module 预测下一对中的第二个 token。训练分两阶段：先做压缩表示对齐，通过 distillation 让压缩向量落到主干可理解的表示空间；再端到端联合训练 Compressor、主干和 MTP module，用离散 token 级 CE 约束两个输出位置。推理时加入 confidence-based adaptive inference：当 MTP 对第二个 token 不够确定时，回退到标准单 token 解码。这个设计牺牲一部分理论上的 2 倍步数压缩，换取难步骤上的准确率稳定。","experiments":"实验使用 Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct、7B-Instruct、14B-Instruct 做 finetuning，评测 MATH500、AMC23、OlympiadBench 和 GPQA-Diamond。论文报告在保持 accuracy 距离标准 CoT baseline 约 1–2 个百分点的情况下，CoT 长度减少约 20–35%。这个数字低于理想的 50%，原因是 adaptive fallback 会在低置信步骤恢复单 token 解码。关键实验应关注不同模型规模下的长度-准确率折中、Compressor 形式的消融、两阶段训练是否必要、以及 confidence threshold 对吞吐和准确率的影响。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 15–25 分钟。重点看方法部分 3.1–3.3：Compressor 如何构造、MTP head 如何接到主干上、adaptive inference 的回退条件是什么。实验部分优先看主结果表和消融表，尤其是线性 Compressor 与 Transformer Compressor 的差异、没有 distillation 时是否掉点、fallback 比例与准确率的关系。需要带着一个限制去读：它主要压缩已有 CoT token 序列，并不证明模型学到了更抽象的连续推理；性能也依赖高质量 CoT 监督和数学指令模型初始化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the serial cost of long chain-of-thought reasoning. Standard autoregressive CoT spends one forward pass per reasoning token, while unconstrained latent reasoning often loses dense token-level supervision and becomes hard to train.","importance":"The main value is as a modeling interface rather than a full pretraining recipe. SuperThoughts sits between explicit CoT and latent reasoning: it compresses adjacent CoT tokens into one representation, but still supervises both discrete tokens with cross-entropy. This makes it useful for studying how far reasoning traces can be compressed before supervision and controllability degrade. The contribution is moderate and is best read as a practical variant of multi-token prediction for reasoning.","technical_approach":"The method groups the CoT span into token pairs. A Compressor maps two token embeddings into one hidden-size vector. The compressor can be a linear projection over concatenated embeddings or a small Transformer block whose second-position hidden state is used as the compressed representation. Given the compressed state, the main backbone predicts the first token of the next pair, and a lightweight MTP module predicts the second token. Training has two stages: first, representation distillation aligns the compressed latent with a space the backbone can consume; then all components are trained jointly with token-level cross-entropy for both predicted positions. At inference time, a confidence-based adaptive decoder falls back to standard single-token generation when the MTP prediction is uncertain. This keeps difficult steps safer, at the cost of giving up the ideal 2x reduction in decoding steps.","experiments":"The paper fine-tunes Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct, 7B-Instruct, and 14B-Instruct, and evaluates on MATH500, AMC23, OlympiadBench, and GPQA-Diamond. It reports roughly 20–35% CoT length reduction while staying within about 1–2 accuracy points of the standard CoT baseline. The reduction is smaller than the theoretical 50% because adaptive fallback restores single-token decoding on uncertain steps. The most informative results are the scale comparison, compressor ablations, the effect of the distillation stage, and the accuracy-throughput curve under different confidence thresholds.","reading_guide":"Skim in 15–25 minutes. Mobile neural processing units (NPUs) offer high-throughput dense matrix computation, but efficiently exploiting them remains challenging: token commitment shrinks per-block effective workloads, token NPU-","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-11T12:44:57Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2606_13740.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 LM / masked LM 作为 AR 替代范式的人，这类系统约束会反过来影响模型训练时应优化什么，例如更少 revision、更稳定 block acceptance、更适合静态 shape 的 denoising 过程。","technical_approach":"llada.cpp 的核心思路是把 block-wise dLLM 的并行计算形态对齐到移动 NPU 的执行特性，而不是简单把 acceptance，避免改变原始提交语义。第二，Dual-Path Progressive Revision：dLLM 中已提交 token 可能随上下文变化被修订，这会造成稀疏、不规则的 KV 更新；论文把常规 dense 计算留在 NPU，把需要细粒度 revision 的 token 识别、更新和 logits 计算交给 CPU 侧专门 kernel，减少对 NPU 静态图执行的干扰。第三，Swap-Optimized Memory Runtime：针对移动 NPU 可见地址空间小的问题，利用计算图生命周期和访问顺序构造更紧凑的虚拟地址布局，减少碎片和 remapping，并将数据 staging 与 NPU execution pipeline 化，尽量隐藏搬运开销。","experiments":"实验对象包括 LLaDA-8B 和多种 dLLM 稳定性，推理系统会更容易吃到并行收益。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets efficient on-device inference for diffusion LLMs on smartphones. dLLMs expose parallelism by denoising multiple tokens within a block, but repeated denoising creates heavy dense transformer computation. The practical bottlenecks are more specific: the effective workload shrinks as tokens are committed, token revision makes KV-cache reuse irregular, and the limited NPU-visible address space causes expensive remapping and data movement.","importance":"The contribution is mainly on the deployment side, but it is relevant to diffusion-LM research. It shows that the latency advantage of non-autoregressive generation depends on more than the number of denoising steps. Block size, commitment policy, revision frequency, KV-cache semantics, and hardware-friendly shape stability all matter. These constraints can feed back into model design and training objectives for diffusion LMs: a model that revises fewer committed tokens and maintains stable block acceptance is easier to accelerate.","technical_approach":"llada.cpp is an NPU-aware inference runtime for block-wise dLLM decoding. It has three main components. Multi-Block Speculative Decoding keeps the NPU busy in late denoising stages by speculatively adding tokens from future blocks while preserving the original left-to-right block acceptance order. Dual-Path Progressive Revision handles the irregular part of token revision on the CPU with specialized kernels, while keeping dense regular computation on the NPU. Swap-Optimized Memory Runtime addresses limited NPU-visible memory by using graph-level lifetime and access-order information to create compact virtual-address layouts, reduce remapping, and pipeline data staging with NPU execution.","experiments":"The evaluation covers LLaDA-8B and multiple dLLM workloads on mobile hardware. The most informative results are likely the latency breakdowns and ablations for speculative multi-block execution, revision handling, and memory-runtime optimization.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 minutes. 1.0，且无 NaN。单 GEMM 在 RTX 3090 上相比 bf16 提速 2.8–4.2 倍，相比未融合 int8 路径提速 4–8 倍。端到端生成在 768px 上提升约 9–10%。1024px 生成可在单张 RTX 3090 上运行，达到 156.5 秒/图，快于先前报告中的 FP8 172.9 秒/图、NF4 164.5 秒/图和 fake-quant INT8 184–185 It separates repeated-trial reliability from the more familiar issue of judge bias.","importance":"The main relevance to pretraining and post-training research is signal quality. LLM judges are now used for leaderboards, preference data construction, reward-model proxies, and RLAIF-style pipelines. If a judge flips pairwise decisions at a non-trivial rate, single-run preferences should not be treated as clean labels. This matters for both evaluation validity and reward data curation. The practical implication is to report uncertainty, repeat trials when conclusions are close, and model judge outputs as noisy measurements rather than deterministic truth.","technical_approach":"The paper defines four layers of judge behavior: pairwise verdicts, pointwise scalar scores, intra-judge consistency under repeated trials, and cross-judge agreement. In pairwise mode, the judge returns A, B, or tie. In pointwise mode, it assigns scores on a 1–10 scale. The key reliability metric is flip rate, the fraction of repeated pairwise trials that do not support the majority outcome. The paper also introduces the pairwise–pointwise gap: cases where forced-choice judgments look decisive while the judge’s own scalar scores show only a small or statistically indistinguishable quality gap.","experiments":"The study uses 29 tasks across 10 categories, including writing, reasoning, coding, knowledge, math, roleplay, extraction, ethics, instruction following, and hard or ambiguous questions. It evaluates two OpenAI judges, GPT-4o-mini and GPT-4.1-mini, with 50 pairwise trials and 50 pointwise trials per question, plus temperature and prompt-sensitivity ablations. The average pairwise flip rate is 13.6%; 28% of questions exceed a 20% flip rate; one question reaches 56%. GPT-4o-mini shows a significant first-position bias, with 72% A-majority and p=0.024. Mean pointwise score gaps are small, around 0.19–0.36 on a 10-point scale, and are not statistically significant. Majority voting helps, but with diminishing returns: on this dataset, about 11 trials are needed on average to recover the 50-trial reference verdict with 95% probability.","reading_guide":"Skim time: 20–30 minutes. The reliability-curve analysis is the most actionable part, because it translates judge noise into trial-count recommendations. Despite their interactive real-world use, existing benchmarks evaluate them as fully-autonomous systems. In this work, we introduce Dialogue SWE-Bench, an automatic benchmark dataset for evaluating the ability of coding agents to resolve real-world software engineering problems through dialogue with a user. eval","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T00:30:33Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_13995.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题很明确：现有 SWE-Bench 一类代码 agent 评测，把任务设成“拿到完整 issue 后自主修复仓库”，但真实使用里，问题描述常常不完整，用户会不断补充、纠正、拒绝方案。于是现有 benchmark 测到的主要是自治修复能力，而不是交互式澄清、需求对齐、边做边问的能力。论文把 repository-level 软件修复任务重写成 dialogue-driven setting，核心不是再造一个 coding benchmark，而是把“对话是否帮助 agent 获得缺失规格、减少误解、最终产出可通过测试的 patch”变成可自动评测的问题。","importance":"对基座预训练本身不是直接方法论文，但对 post-train、agent training、以及评测信号设计有现实意义。它指出一个常被忽略的断层：更强的代码模型，不一定就是更好的交互式 coding agent。这个结论会影响两类研究。第一类是 agent SFT/RL 数据构造：如果训练数据几乎都是单轮、规格完备的 coding tasks，模型就学不会主动澄清。第二类是 evaluator 设计：只看最终 patch pass rate，会掩盖 agent 在对话阶段的失误模式。贡献量级属于“评测设定补齐短板”，不是改变预训练范式，但对做 coding agent 或 tool-use post-training 的团队有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法上有三块。第一块是 benchmark 重构。作者从 SWE-Bench Verified 出发，不再把完整 issue text 直接给 agent，而是为每个任务设计一个固定的初始用户 query，故意只暴露部分信息。agent 的动作空间在原有编程环境操作之外，新增 message_user 动作，允许它向用户提问并接收回复。这样任务成功不只取决于代码能力，也取决于是否知道什么时候问、问什么、如何根据回答更新计划。\n\n第二块是 user simulator。论文没有用一个无约束聊天模型来扮演用户，而是做了 persona-grounded simulator：用户回复受完整 issue text 约束，并带有角色设定，目的是同时保证两点，一是回答要忠实于原始任务规格，二是交互风格要更像真实用户，而不是把标准答案一次性吐给 agent。这里的关键设计不是“让模拟器更聪明”，而是“让模拟器更稳定、可复现、不会泄露超出用户视角的信息”。从 benchmark 角度，这比开放式人类评测更便宜，也比自由生成更可控。\n\n第三块是 schema-guided agent。作者提出一个受 dialogue theory 启发的 agent 变体，用显式 schema 约束 agent 在对话中的行为，例如区分澄清需求、确认假设、汇报进展、请求决策等不同交互意图。它不是单纯在 prompt 里加一句“多问用户”，而是把对话动作组织成更结构化的决策过程。这样做的原因很实际：通用 coding agent 往往把对话当成普通文本输出，结果要么几乎不问，要么问得泛、问得晚、问完也不用。schema 的作用是把“何时问、为何问、问完怎么改计划”显式化。","experiments":"实验重点有三层。第一层是 benchmark 上的主结果。作者评测了多种现有 coding models / agents，结论是模型在传统 coding benchmark 上更强，并不自动转化为 dialogue setting 下更强。这说明交互能力不是代码能力的自然副产物，而是单独需要训练和评测的维度。\n\n第二层是 schema-guided agent 的增益。论文给出的摘要数字是，相比强基线提升 3% 到 14%。从任务性质看，这个幅度不算小，因为 repository-level SWE 任务本来通过率就低，几个点的绝对提升通常对应不少真实任务被从“误解需求”拉回到“可修复”。更重要的是，提升来自更好的提问与对齐，而不只是更强的 patch generation。\n\n第三层是 dialogue quality evaluation。作者除了最终 resolved rate，还引入自动化对话质量评估，并用 LLM-as-a-Judge 去看提问是否相关、是否有帮助、是否冗余等。这个设计的价值在于把失败拆开：有的 agent 代码改得对但不会沟通，有的 agent 会沟通但问不到关键点，有的 agent 过度依赖用户。论文还讨论了 better coding models do not always correspond to better interactive agents，这个观察本身比单个分数更重要。\n\n从给出的全文片段看，数据集基于 SWE-Bench Verified 重构，评测核心仍是最终 patch 通过测试的 resolved percentage，同时叠加多种 dialogue-level 指标。若精读原文，建议重点核对：任务数规模、user simulator 的温度与稳定性设置、不同 agent 的 message frequency 分布、以及 dialogue quality 与 task success 的相关性分析。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，抓住它为什么认为 fully-autonomous SWE evaluation 不够。这里有两个关键事实：大量 issue 本身欠规格；真实用户会频繁纠正 agent，但 agent 很少主动澄清。然后看 §3.2 和 §3.3，理解 benchmark 如何从 SWE-Bench Verified 改写成交互环境，以及 simulator 如何被约束，避免信息泄露和高方差。\n\n如果只花 20 到 30 分钟，最值得看的部分是：Figure 2 的任务流程、user simulator 设计、主表里不同 agent 在 dialogue setting 的排名变化、以及 schema-guided agent 相对 baseline 的增益。若再多花一点时间，建议看自动 dialogue evaluation 的定义，因为这部分决定了 benchmark 是否只是在“多加一个聊天接口”，还是确实能测到交互质量。\n\n值得深挖的点有三个。第一，初始 query 的信息遮蔽程度如何控制；遮蔽太少，dialogue 变成装饰，遮蔽太多，又可能把任务变成猜谜。第二，simulator 是否会把 benchmark 变成“对 simulator prompt 的适配”。第三，schema-guided agent 的收益到底来自结构化对话，还是来自更长、更谨慎的 prompting。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a clear gap in coding-agent evaluation: existing SWE benchmarks mostly assume a fully specified issue and measure autonomous repository repair, while real usage is interactive and often underspecified. In practice, users clarify requirements, reject wrong assumptions, and steer the agent mid-trajectory. The paper reformulates repository-level software repair into a dialogue-driven benchmark, so the key question becomes whether an agent can ask useful questions, recover missing specification through interaction, and still produce a test-passing patch.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for post-training, agent training, and evaluation design. If agent SFT/RL data mostly contains single-turn, fully specified coding tasks, the model has little incentive to learn clarification behavior. It also matters for evaluation: patch success alone hides whether the agent failed because of coding, planning, or dialogue. Evaluating Language-Switching Invariance in LLM-as-a-Judge","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) are now widely used as automatic judges for open-ended instruction-following evaluation. This practice is convenient, scalable, and often more semantically aware than reference-based metrics, but it also introduces a new reliability question: does a judge evaluate the quality of an answer, or does it also react to the language in which the comparison is presented? We propose Judge-LS, a lightweight meta-evaluation protocol that transforms LLMBar response-pair items i","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T08:59:26Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_14278.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究的是 LLM-as-a-Judge 的一个具体可靠性问题：当同一组 instruction 和候选答案被翻译成另一种语言，或者变成中英混写后，judge 的偏好是否应保持不变。若答案质量关系没有变，judge 却因为呈现语言不同而改判，说明它评的并不只是内容质量，还混入了语言偏好。论文把这个问题形式化为 language-switching invariance，并给出一个低成本、可复现实验协议 Judge-LS。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关系不如训练方法论文强，但它对评测基础设施很重要，尤其是多语言 instruction-following、LLM judge、合成数据筛选、RLHF/RLAIF 奖励模型替代等场景。现在很多团队用 judge 做模型选择、数据过滤、偏好打分，默认 judge 是“语义等价不变”的。若这个假设不成立，多语言实验结论、reward 信号、甚至 leaderboard 排名都可能被表层语言因素污染。它的贡献不在提出新模型，而在把一个常被忽略的评测偏差拆成可测的指标体系。","technical_approach":"方法设计相当干净。作者基于 LLMBar 的 419 个 pairwise instruction-following 样本，构造三种版本：原始英文 EN、完整中文 ZH、以及保留部分术语和实体的中英混写 LS。关键要求是 label-preserving translation，也就是翻译不能修复原答案中的事实错误、格式错误、数学错误或 instruction-following 错误，否则 gold label 会失效。为此他们用 gpt-4.1-mini 做转换，并加了自动审计，检查空字段、长度异常收缩、数字 token 数变化等高风险样本，再做敏感性分析。\n\n评测协议不只看普通 accuracy。每个 item 在每种语言条件下都做 answer-order swap，避免把位置偏差误当语言偏差。judge 输出 A/B/Tie 后再映射回原始 response ID。这样每个样本会产生多次判断。除此之外，作者还设计了 translation-equivalence tie probes：把 gold answer 的英文版和中文版互相比，理论上应判 Tie。若 judge 系统性偏向英文或中文，就会在这个 probe 上暴露出来。\n\n指标体系也比常见 judge 评测更细。除了 strict accuracy 和 tie-half accuracy，还看 language-invariance flip rate、gold-correctness flip rate、position inconsistency、source-level robustness、language preference、uncertainty estimates、paired significance tests 和 usage cost。这里最有价值的是把“判错”与“因语言切换而改判”分开。一个 judge 可以准确率尚可，但 invariance 很差；这对实际使用同样危险。","experiments":"实验覆盖 4 个 API judge，在完整 419-item 数据上共得到 13,408 次成功判断。这个规模不算大，但对一个 focused meta-evaluation 已足够，而且设计上把每个 item 的多种语言、顺序、tie probe 都系统展开了。\n\n从全文给出的结果框架看，作者主要比较几类现象。第一，标准准确率在不同语言条件下是否稳定。第二，同一 item 在 EN、ZH、LS 三种呈现下是否发生 preference flip。第三，在 gold-answer translation tie probe 上，judge 是否偏向某种语言而不是给 Tie。第四，去掉 19 个高风险翻译样本后，结论是否稳健。\n\n这类实验最关键的不是单个 accuracy 数字，而是误差结构。如果某个 judge 在英文上准确率高，但一旦切到中文或中英混写就频繁 flip，说明它不适合作为多语言评测器；如果它在 translation-equivalent probe 上持续偏好英文，则说明 reward 或 ranking 可能带有语言歧视。论文摘要没有给出每个模型的完整数值排名，但从设计看，作者已经把“语言不变性”从模糊担忧变成了可量化诊断。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，30 分钟左右能抓住主要价值。先看 Introduction，明确它不是在做多语言生成 benchmark，而是在审计 judge 的稳定性。然后重点看 Judge-LS Protocol 和 Metrics。前者决定实验是否公平，后者决定你能否区分 accuracy、position bias、language bias、tie handling 这些不同问题。\n\n如果时间有限，最值得看的内容有四处：一是三种语言版本 EN/ZH/LS 的构造原则，尤其是如何保持 label；二是 answer-order swap 和 tie probe 的设计；三是主结果表里不同 judge 的 flip rate 与 language preference；四是 sensitivity analysis，确认结论不是由少数翻译异常样本驱动。\n\n值得深挖的点有两个。第一，使用 gpt-4.1-mini 做翻译会不会把某些风格偏差带进 benchmark；作者已做机械审计，但语义层面的 label preservation 仍有残余风险。第二，英文-中文只是一个起点，真正部署里还会遇到 code-mixed、低资源语言、专业术语密集场景，Judge-LS 的协议可以直接扩展到这些条件。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific reliability issue in LLM-as-a-Judge: whether a judge preserves its preference when the same instruction-response pair is translated into another language or presented in a Chinese-English mixed form. If answer quality is unchanged but the judgment flips, then the judge is reacting to surface language rather than only to response quality. The paper formalizes this as language-switching invariance and proposes a lightweight evaluation protocol called Judge-LS.","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining is indirect but real. Many pipelines now use LLM judges for model comparison, synthetic data filtering, preference labeling, and reward approximation. Those workflows often assume semantic invariance under language changes. If that assumption fails, multilingual evaluation, reward signals, and leaderboard conclusions can all be distorted by presentation language. Beyond documented overconfidence, a more severe underconfidence pattern dominates: agents flag correct answers as incorrect. Standard outcome-only reinforcement learning (RL) cannot resolve this flaw due to structural credit-assignment mismatch, and even trains agents to suppress honest error flags.","importance":"A previously overlooked structural flaw in mainstream agentic RL pipelines is exposed: pipelines optimize only for task outcomes without calibrating post-feedback self-assessment. The proposed calibration mechanism requires no additional annotation, reward model, or LLM judge, can be directly integrated into popular RL algorithms like GRPO, and improves both task performance and calibration. Calibrated reflections also enable pseudo-reward based self-improvement without outcome supervision and test-time selective prediction, offering actionable insights for reward signal design in agent post-training.","technical_approach":"The RefGRPO algorithm extends standard outcome-only RL with two core components: 1) A free calibration bonus: during RL training, both the agent's post-feedback binary reflection score \\(s^{\\text{ref}}\\) and ground-truth task outcome \\(r\\) are already available. An indicator bonus \\(c = \\mathbb{I}(s^{\\text{ref}} = r)\\) is added to the outcome reward before group normalization, granting higher relative advantage to well-calibrated rollouts regardless of task success. 2) A dynamic schedule for the calibration coefficient \\(\\alpha(t)\\): initialized to a large value to prioritize early calibration learning, then decayed gradually to shift focus to task performance while retaining calibration ability. The ChowScore from statistical learning is introduced as a unified metric for joint evaluation of task accuracy and reflection calibration.","experiments":"Experiments are conducted on a multi-turn text-to-SQL setup with Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct. We identify the cause as the double-drift phenomenon: optimizing a variational surrogate can let the ELBO separate from the true log-likelihood, which then makes the resulting proxy policy gradient misaligned with the true policy gradient of expected return. We propose \\textbf{DiPOD}, a diffusion polic","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-11T18:06:04Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_13795.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决 diffusion policy 做 RL post-training 时的梯度可靠性问题。diffusion 模型的真实 log-likelihood 通常不可 tractable，现有方法用 ELBO、EUBO、mean-field、一阶估计等 proxy 代替；但 RL 更新会让这些 variational proxy 与真实 likelihood 的差距变大，进一步导致 proxy policy gradient 偏离真实 policy gradient。论文把这个耦合失效称为 double drift。","importance":"它对 diffusion LLM 的 post-train 有直接启发。AR 模型做 RL 时可以直接用 token logprob；dLLM 若用 variational surrogate 做策略梯度，不能只看 reward 是否上升，还要维护生成模型自身的概率结构。论文提出的关键信号是：pretraining 形成的 tight-bound 区域在 RL 中会被破坏，需要把“保持 likelihood proxy 可信”作为训练约束。这个问题也连接 pretrain-posttrain 接口：预训练目标越接近后续 RL 所用的 surrogate，post-training 越稳定；但 RL 过程中仍需要持续校准 surrogate。","technical_approach":"DiPOD 的原则是把 policy improvement 和 tight-bound maintenance 交替进行。理论部分先定义一类 adequate proxy gradient：如果 evidence bound 与真实 likelihood 足够贴合，那么基于 proxy 的策略梯度方向与真实策略梯度一致。问题在于普通 RL 更新不会保持这种贴合。DiPOD 用 policy-preserving self-distillation 把模型拉回 tight-bound regime，再执行 policy-improving gradient update。实际算法做了一个简单近似：每次 diffusion policy-gradient update 都在同一批 on-policy rollout 上加入 ELBO maximization regularizer。这个 ELBO 项不是普通的预训练损失复用，而是为了在当前策略分布附近降低 variational discrepancy，使 proxy gradient 不至于随着训练偏离真实目标。","experiments":"实验覆盖离散 diffusion language model post-training 和连续控制 flow policy。离散任务包括 GSM8K、MATH500、Sudoku、Countdown；连续控制包括 humanoid motion tracking。给出的结论是，加入 on-policy ELBO regularizer 后，训练稳定性和 reward optimization 都有明显改善。当前材料没有提供具体准确率、reward 数值或 ablation 表格，因此不应过度解读幅度。需要重点查看完整实验中的三类证据：ELBO-log-likelihood gap 或 proxy tightness 的变化、reward 曲线稳定性、ELBO regularizer 权重和 on-policy/off-policy 数据选择的消融。","reading_guide":"建议泛读到半精读，预计 35 分钟。先看 double drift 的定义和推导，确认 ELBO drift 如何传导到 policy-gradient drift；再看 DiPOD 的 interleaving 形式和 practical algorithm，重点是为什么 self-distillation 被称为 policy-preserving，以及为什么实现上可以退化为 on-policy ELBO regularization；最后看 dLLM reasoning 任务和 continuous-control 任务的实验曲线。对做 dLLM RL 的读者，最值得深挖的是 surrogate tightness 的监控指标和 regularizer 系数如何随训练阶段调整。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why reinforcement-learning post-training is unstable for diffusion policies. Diffusion models usually do not provide a tractable exact log-likelihood, so existing RL methods optimize policy-gradient proxies based on ELBO, EUBO, mean-field estimates, one-step estimates, or imposed dependency structures. The paper identifies a coupled failure mode: RL updates loosen the variational bound, and once the bound drifts away from the true log-likelihood, the proxy policy gradient also drifts away from the true policy-gradient direction.","importance":"This is directly relevant to diffusion LLM post-training. Autoregressive LMs expose token log probabilities, but diffusion LMs often rely on variational surrogates. Reward improvement alone is not enough if the surrogate no longer represents the executed diffusion policy. The useful lesson is that post-training must preserve the probabilistic structure inherited from pretraining. It also clarifies a pretrain-to-posttrain interface issue: objectives that are tight near initialization can become unreliable under RL unless tightness is actively maintained.","technical_approach":"DiPOD enforces two requirements: improve the downstream return and keep the likelihood proxy tight. The analysis defines adequate proxy gradients, where the proxy gradient matches the true likelihood gradient when the evidence bound is tight. Since standard RL updates do not preserve this condition, DiPOD alternates policy-preserving self-distillation with policy-improving gradient updates. The practical version is simple: add an on-policy ELBO maximization term to each diffusion policy-gradient update on the same rollout batch. This regularizer reduces variational discrepancy around the current policy distribution, improving alignment between the proxy gradient and the intended policy gradient.","experiments":"The experiments cover discrete diffusion-LM post-training tasks, including GSM8K, MATH500, Sudoku, and Countdown, as well as continuous-control flow policies such as humanoid motion tracking. The reported outcome is improved training stability and better reward optimization after adding the on-policy ELBO regularizer. The provided material does not include exact accuracy or reward numbers. The key evidence to inspect in the full results is the evolution of proxy tightness, reward curves, and ablations on the ELBO regularization strength and rollout distribution.","reading_guide":"Skim with selective deep reading in about 35 minutes. Read the double-drift analysis first, especially the link between ELBO-log-likelihood discrepancy and policy-gradient misalignment. Then read the DiPOD algorithm and check how the ideal interleaving is approximated by an on-policy ELBO regularizer. The most useful experimental sections are the dLLM reasoning tasks, the continuous-control transfer of the same principle, and the ablations on regularization. For diffusion-LM RL, the main implementation question is how to monitor surrogate tightness during training.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"非 AR LM 接 RL 的关键坑在目标漂移；先看 double-drift 诊断和 ELBO 正则消融。","en":"For RL on non-AR LMs, the useful part is the objective-drift diagnosis; read the double-drift analysis and ELBO-regularizer ablations first."},"metadata":{"institutions":["UC Berkeley"],"authors":["Haozhe Jiang","Haiwen Feng","Pieter Abbeel","Jiantao Jiao","Angjoo Kanazawa","Nika Haghtalab"],"notable_authors":["Pieter Abbeel","Jiantao Jiao","Angjoo Kanazawa","Nika Haghtalab"],"tags":["diffusion-lm","rl-post-training","policy-gradient","training-stability"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13795","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.13795","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.14691","title":"CORA: Analyzing and bridging thinking-answer gap in Multimodal RLVR via Consistency-Oriented Reasoning Alignment","abstract":"Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has successfully elicited the reasoning capabilities of large language models, motivating its extension to multimodal scenarios. mod","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T17:54:59Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_14691.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在多模态 answer”。它不看最终对错，而是评估推理链与答案的蕴含/支持关系，输出连续分数作为奖励。\n2) HRAS（Hybrid Reward Advantage Splitting）：为了解决 GRPO 的 group-wise normalization 在混合离散（正确性）与连续（一致性）奖励时可能导致信号互相干扰的问题，把两种奖励分开做组内归一化与 advantage answer: the answer can be correct while the <think> content does not entail/support it or even contradicts it. The paper studies why this persists throughout GRPO training and proposes a training signal to align reasoning with answers.","importance":"More relevant to the RL-to-foundation-model feedback loop than to pure pretraining. The key lesson is that answer-only discrete rewards leave the reasoning channel underconstrained, making it exploitable: the policy can optimize correctness while letting the chain-of-thought drift. This matters if RLVR rollouts are later reused for distillation or continual training, because it can inject unfaithful CoT into the data distribution. CORA offers a relatively lightweight fix: add a learnable consistency reward and prevent it from being washed out by group-wise normalization in GRPO.","technical_approach":"CORA (Consistency-Oriented Reasoning Alignment) has two components:\n1) CRM (Consistency Reward Model): a plug-and-play discriminative model that scores whether the reasoning semantically supports the answer, framed like NLI (entailment/support vs. mismatch). It provides a continuous reward independent of answer correctness.\n2) HRAS (Hybrid Reward Advantage Splitting): since GRPO uses group-wise normalization, mixing discrete accuracy rewards with continuous consistency rewards can cause interference. HRAS computes normalized advantages separately for task reward and consistency reward, then combines them with weights, preserving both preference signals (be correct, and be consistent).\nDesign choice: instead of expensive external verifiers or complex reward stacks, use a lightweight CRM and modify advantage estimation rather than the core optimizer, aiming for stability and ease of integration.","experiments":"Two main experimental threads:\n- Training-dynamics analysis: collect on-policy rollouts during GRPO, define a binary consistency label and report Inconsistency Rate (IR). This lack of targeted control makes it difficult to design and implement reliable safety controls. To understand these side-effects, we introduce a diagnostic framework for analyzing interacting behaviors in LLMs. We model behaviors as low-rank subspaces in activation space, and study how interventions influence across behaviors. Across multiple inst","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T12:24:25Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_14388.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决的是 LLM 安全干预的可控性问题：针对拒答、越狱、谄媚等单一行为做微调或 activation steering 时，为什么经常会改动其他行为，以及这种副作用能否在表示空间里提前诊断。关键点不在于再提出一个新的干预算法，而是把“行为不是彼此独立的控制旋钮”这件事形式化，并解释干预影响为何具有方向性。","importance":"对预训练和 post-train 接口有直接启发。许多安全、对齐和偏好训练方法默认可以局部修改某类行为，但该工作显示，不同行为在 residual stream 中共享低秩表示结构；一个行为的干预如果落在共享子空间上，就可能改变另一个行为。更重要的是，副作用不是对称的，A 影响 B 不等于 B 影响 A。这提示后训练阶段的行为调控不能只看目标任务指标，也要测量行为子空间的重叠、决策耦合和传播路径。贡献量级偏诊断框架和机制分析，不是训练 recipe，但对设计更可控的 alignment objective、orthogonality constraint、activation intervention 有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法从 matched contrastive dataset 开始。对每个行为 m 构造正负样本对，正样本诱发该行为，负样本抑制该行为，并尽量匹配非行为因素。模型表示取 decoder-only Transformer 某一层 residual stream 的 last-token activation。对每个正负样本对计算差分向量 Δ_i = h_last(x_i^+) - h_last(x_i^-)，把所有差分堆成矩阵，中心化后做 SVD。不同于只取单个“行为方向”的做法，作者把 top-k principal components 作为行为低秩子空间，用它描述该行为在激活空间中的主要变化模式。\n\n一个重要处理是移除 decision-aligned variance。初始 SVD 的主成分可能主要反映模型最终选择正/负标签的决策信号，而不是更细的行为结构。作者用 label string 的 log-probability margin 定义决策边界相关信号，识别与 margin 高相关的方向，并将这部分显式剥离，再分析剩余的行为子空间。这样做的动机是避免把“模型已经决定输出什么”误当成“行为内部表示结构”。\n\n干预分析有两个核心量：第一是行为子空间之间的几何重叠，通常可通过投影、principal angle 或相关的 overlap metric 衡量；第二是 decision coupling，即某个行为子空间与决策子空间之间的角度或耦合强度。重叠解释“一个行为的干预为什么能碰到另一个行为”，决策耦合解释“碰到以后为什么会真正改变输出”。两者结合后可以解释非对称性：如果 A 的子空间既与 B 重叠，又更强地接入模型决策，那么 A 上的干预会更容易传播到 B；反过来未必成立。作者用 projection-based intervention 来验证这一点，即对源行为子空间做投影、消除或 steering，然后测量目标行为的 margin 或行为指标变化。","experiments":"实验覆盖多个 instruction-tuned 模型，规模从 7B 到 70B，行为设置包括 refusal、jailbreak 和 sycophancy。主要结果有四点。第一，拒答、越狱、谄媚等行为在激活空间中并非完全分离，存在可测量的共享低秩结构。第二，单方向行为表示不足以解释干预副作用，低秩子空间能更稳定地刻画跨行为传播。第三，cross-behavior effect 与子空间 overlap 和 decision coupling 同时相关；只有 overlap 高但决策耦合弱时，干预未必显著改变目标行为。第四，干预影响有明显方向性，A→B 和 B→A 的强度不同，一些行为更像上游控制点，改动后会广泛影响其他行为，而另一些行为的影响更局部。\n\n给出的材料中没有完整列出各模型、各行为对的具体表格数值，因此不应把结果理解为某个固定提升幅度。更稳妥的读法是：7B–70B 多尺度模型上的现象一致，说明该诊断框架不是某个小模型或单一数据集的偶然结果。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Section 3 和 Section 5。Section 3 的重点是 matched contrast construction、SVD 子空间抽取、decision margin、decision-aligned variance removal，以及 overlap / decision coupling 的定义。这里决定了整个分析是否可靠。Section 5 应重点看跨行为干预矩阵、A→B 与 B→A 的非对称结果、不同层和不同 k 的敏感性，以及“upstream control point”的具体例子。\n\n读的时候可以重点检查三个问题。第一，正负样本对是否真的控制了非行为因素，否则 SVD 主方向可能捕捉主题、格式或语气。第二，移除 decision-aligned variance 后，剩余子空间是否仍能预测行为干预效果。第三，projection intervention 的强度是否可比，因为不同子空间维度和激活范数会影响干预幅度。若要把该方法用于自己的安全训练或 post-train 诊断，最值得复现的是行为子空间 overlap heatmap 和方向性干预矩阵。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a control problem in LLM safety interventions: when fine-tuning or activation steering targets one behavior, such as refusal, jailbreak susceptibility, or sycophancy, why do other behaviors change as a side effect? The main goal is not to propose another intervention method, but to build a representation-level diagnostic for predicting and explaining these cross-behavior effects.","importance":"The work is relevant to the interface between pretraining, instruction tuning, and safety post-training. Many alignment interventions implicitly treat behaviors as separable control targets. This paper shows that several behaviors share low-rank structure in the residual stream, so modifying one behavior can move the model along directions that also support other behaviors. The asymmetry result is especially useful: an intervention from behavior A to behavior B need not match the reverse direction. This matters for safety evaluations, activation steering, preference tuning, and attempts to impose orthogonality constraints between behavioral features. The contribution is best viewed as a diagnostic and mechanistic framework rather than a new training recipe.","technical_approach":"$2e","experiments":"The experiments cover multiple instruction-tuned models from 7B to 70B parameters and several behavior settings, including refusal, jailbreak, and sycophancy. The main empirical findings are qualitative but consistent. First, different behaviors share internal low-rank structure. Second, low-rank subspaces explain cross-behavior effects better than isolated one-dimensional directions. Third, cross-behavior interference is associated with both subspace overlap and decision coupling. High overlap alone is not sufficient if the shared representation is weakly coupled to the model’s decision. Fourth, intervention effects are directional: A→B can be strong while B→A is weak. Some behaviors behave like upstream control points whose interventions propagate broadly, while others remain more localized.\n\nThe provided content does not include the full numerical tables for every model and behavior pair, so the safest interpretation is about the repeated pattern across 7B–70B models rather than a single headline number.","reading_guide":"Read Section 3 carefully. Adversarial Concept Search (ACS)：给定一组概念（如实体、关系、语言、推理子任务等），先为每个“原子概念”构造其在模型中的线性表示方向，然后用这些方向之间的几何关系预测“组合后是否会失败”。论文的机制假设是“有损超位置导致的组合干扰”：理论上在k-稀疏激活下，非正交特征也可被无损解码；但真实LLM的解码是有损的，当多个任务相关特征同时激活且其线性方向接近时，会发生干扰，导致组合失败。落地上，作者在不同任务里定义了不同的‘原子表示’抽取方式：\n- 在受控的组合基准（SCAN）里，概念更接近离散指令/原语，便于测量概念对的交互与角度，并把角度与组合错误率做相关分析。\n- 在多跳QA里，把两跳问题拆成两个单跳子问题，分别得到“单跳表示”，再用两者的夹角/相似度预测两跳组合是否成功。\n- 在多语事实回忆里，把“事实表示”与“语言子空间/语言方向”的几何关系作为检索/回忆成功率的预测因子。\n整体区别于常见的 error prediction / active learning：后者通常需要具体输入、需要跑模型得到不确定性或损失；ACS试图在‘输入生成之前’只用概念描述与原子表示做排序，从而把成本从‘枚举输入并推理’前移到‘少量探测/抽取表示’。","experiments":"全文主线实验是“角度越大（越接近正交）→组合越可靠；角度越小（方向接近）→更易失败”。作者在三个层级验证：\n1) SCAN 受控实验：在不同数据条件与模型规模下，测得概念对角度与组合泛化错误显著相关，并用来预测哪些组合指令会失败。\n2) 多跳QA：用两个单跳组件的表示几何预测两跳是否答对，展示无需直接评测两跳输入也能提前筛出高风险组合。\n3) MoE Transformer 中的 task-expert specialization：为什么不同任务会被路由到不同专家，以及专家容量如何与任务复杂度相关。","importance":"对 MoE 预训练研究的价值主要在理论直觉。现有许多 MoE 专家分化分析基于连续分布或前馈模型，难以对应自然语言中的模板、实体和事实检索。该工作把问题改写成模板加 key-value dictionary 根据关系结构或模板信息把输入分配给任务相关专家；专家再执行任务内部的 associative retrieval，从对应知识表中取回 object。理论部分包括两个核心结果：一个是 dense Transformer 中可形成稀疏功能 circuit，另一个是 MoE Transformer 可用 task-specific experts can emerge in MoE Transformers under a discrete, language-like data model, rather than under the continuous mixture models used in much of the prior theory.","importance":"Its main value is theoretical intuition for MoE pretraining. Empirical MoE systems often show expert specialization, but existing theoretical explanations usually rely on continuous distributions or feedforward-only models. This work introduces a discrete template-and-dictionary setting that better resembles factual language data. It gives a constructive account of how attention, routing, and expert computation can divide labor. The result is not a practical large-scale training recipe, but it helps clarify why explicit routing supervision can produce cleaner expert-task alignment than a standard objective alone.","technical_approach":"The authors define structured knowledge data using syntactic templates and finite key-value dictionaries. A template contains two wildcard positions, which are instantiated with a subject and an associated object. A task consists of a set of templates and a task-specific knowledge table, and its complexity depends on both the number and length of templates and the size of the dictionary.\n\nWithin this setting, the paper constructs single-layer dense and MoE Transformers. The attention block separates structural information from factual subject information. The router maps inputs to task-specific experts based on relational or template structure. Each expert then performs associative retrieval over the corresponding task dictionary. The theoretical core includes a dense Transformer construction with sparse functional circuits and an MoE construction in which experts specialize by task, with expert size scaling with task complexity.","experiments":"The experiments are conducted on synthetic knowledge datasets. They examine whether learned routing matches the task structure predicted by the theory. The main finding is that explicit task-aware routing objectives yield substantially clearer task-expert alignment than standard objectives alone. Standard training may still produce some specialization, but the mapping from tasks to experts is weaker and less stable. There are no large-scale real-language MoE experiments in the provided content, so the empirical claim should be read as support for the theoretical model rather than as evidence for a production-scale MoE training method.","reading_guide":"A skim read should take about 20–30 minutes. Focus on Section 3 for the template, wildcard, dictionary, and task-complexity definitions. Then read Theorem 4.2 to understand the constructed division of labor between attention, router, and experts. Section 6 is useful for the synthetic routing experiments and the effect of task-aware routing supervision. ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T17:52:24Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_14688.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.DS"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是“如何证明更多定理”，而是一个更靠前的问题：在有完美 proof checker 的前提下，系统究竟能否稳定地产生“有数学价值”的内容，而不是被大量形式上正确但内容空洞的 trivia 淹没。作者把问题形式化为嵌套语言生成：可验证语言 F 由 oracle 给出，真正有价值的子语言 H 不可直接观测，系统只能看到一个由对手按某种顺序枚举出来的已知核心 C⊆H，也就是文献。核心要问的是，在这种设定下，验证器到底提供了什么信息，价值学习又需要什么条件，以及“广覆盖”与“避免 trivia”之间是否存在不可消除的张力。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发不在数学自动化本身，而在“可验证信号不等于高质量训练信号”这件事被做成了严格结论。它把 proof checker、compiler、unit test、reward model 这类外部可验证器的作用边界讲得很清楚：验证器能约束合法性、减少 hallucination，但不能替代 taste，也不能单独决定什么样的输出值得保留。对做 RL pretrain、self-improvement、tool-augmented generation、代码/数学数据合成的人，这个结论很重要，因为它说明只靠 verifier 扩数据，最终会系统性地产生大量“真但没用”的样本。贡献量级更像一篇基础理论澄清：它不提供新算法，但把一个在合成数据和自动探索里反复出现的经验问题，变成了可证明的限制与条件。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者建立了 language generation in the limit 的嵌套版本。宇宙是可数字符串集合 U，实例是 H⊆F⊆U，其中 F 是形式上可验证的环境语言，H 是未知的“有价值语言”。系统每轮看到文献前缀 S_t、可查询 F 的 membership oracle，并输出一个不在 S_t 中的新字符串。输出被分成三类：valuable、trivial、hallucination。评价指标不是有限步精度，而是极限意义下对 H 的覆盖密度，以及输出中 trivia 的长期比例。\n\n全文的四个主结论里，最关键的是前两个。第一，Theorem 3.1 说明 verifier is not taste：对“最终广覆盖 unseen valuable statements”这个目标，带 oracle 与不带 oracle 的可实现性是同一个刻画，仍然由 Angluin 式 tell-tale condition 决定。也就是说，oracle 只能帮助识别 ambient language F，无法在同一个 F 的候选 H 之间提供区分信息。第二，Theorem 3.2 说明 verifier 真正能买到的是 sound coverage：你可以在追求覆盖时保证所有输出都属于 F，从而消除 hallucination，但这仍然不解决 triviality。\n\n后面的结果给出更强的负面边界。作者证明，只要想在未知 H 上获得有 breadth 的生成能力，就必须接受无限多 trivia；不存在一个既能广覆盖又只输出有限个 trivial statement 的机制。更细一点地说，trivia 的频率可以在渐近上变得很低，但其总量不能是有限的，这就是标题里的 flood and harvest：想收获未记录的价值，就必须穿过一片可验证但无价值的洪水。文中还讨论了 exact/approximate breadth、soundness、coverage density 等不同目标之间的关系，并把可实现条件写成集合族层面的结构性质，而不是某个具体算法技巧。","experiments":"这是一篇理论论文，没有常规意义上的训练实验、参数量或 benchmark 分数。全文的“实验性”内容主要是构造性例子和定理边界。引言里给了一个很有代表性的例子：可以轻易生成一个形式上调用 Euler identity、golden ratio、Gamma function、zeta function 等复杂符号、但数学内容仍然只是 1+1=2 的机器可验证命题，用来说明 checker 对“值不值得”没有判断力。\n\n如果从结果强度看，最值得记的是三点。第一，oracle 对 breadth 的帮助严格受限，只在识别 F 上有用，对 H 的 taste 学习无能为力。第二，soundness 可以通过 oracle 获得，也就是可以避免 hallucination。第三，任何想覆盖未见 valuable statements 的生成器，都不可避免地产生无限多 trivia；能优化的是 trivia 的渐近速率，而不是把它消灭到有限次。对做数据合成的人，这相当于一个下界：如果目标空间远大于已知高质量核心，且价值标准不能被 verifier 直接表达，那么“低价值真样本”会是结构性产物，不是工程实现偶然出了问题。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一看 Introduction，作者把问题动机讲得很完整，尤其是 checker certifies validity, not value 这一段，基本定义了全文的阅读坐标。第二看 Theorem 3.1 和 Theorem 3.2 及其前后的定义，理解 oracle 到底提供什么、不提供什么。第三看关于 flood 必然性的主定理和对应图示，那里给出了最有现实映射的结论。\n\n如果时间有限，建议 30–45 分钟读法：先读模型设定，明确 F、H、C、breadth、sound coverage、density 这些对象；再读四个主结论的 statement；最后只挑证明思路里和 tell-tale condition、fiber-wise hand, what can a generator actually guarantee about mathematical value rather than mere formal validity? The setup separates a verifiable formal language F from an unknown valuable sublanguage H. The system only sees an adversarially revealed core C⊆H, meant to model the literature, and can query membership in F through the checker. The central issue is whether verification helps with value, what it can certify, and whether broad discovery of valuable statements can avoid being swamped by formally correct but worthless trivia.","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining is conceptual but concrete. The paper turns a familiar empirical complaint into a formal limitation: verifiers certify correctness, not usefulness. That matters for proof synthesis, code generation with tests, tool-verified data synthesis, RL with external checkers, and any pipeline that treats “verifiable” as a proxy for “good training signal.” The contribution is not a new training method. It is a clean theory result about the boundary of verifier-based supervision. In practice, it argues that scaling generation with a checker alone will systematically produce many true-but-low-value samples unless value is learned from examples or some richer signal.","technical_approach":"$30","experiments":"$31","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and the formal setup. We find that the model develops a specific mechanism for this: a small set of attention heads in its language-model backbone, which we call gaze heads, whose attention tracks the image region the model is currently describing. We find them with a simple correlation score from a few forward passes, using comic strips as a controlled testbed where narrative order is laid out spatially. These gaze hea","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T17:59:57Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_14703.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的是：VLM 在生成描述时，语言 backbone 内部到底是怎样把“当前正在说的文字”对齐到“当前正在看的图像区域”的。作者不是泛泛地找“看图的头”，而是找一类更具体的机制性 attention heads：它们会随着生成过程逐 token 切换关注区域，去跟踪模型此刻正在描述的那一块视觉内容。更进一步，作者要验证这类头不只是相关，而是对“描述哪一块”具有因果控制力。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是核心推进，更接近 VLM 机制分析与可控生成研究。但对做原生多模态预训练的人有两点启发。第一，视觉 grounding 在语言 backbone 里可能被压缩到一小簇中后层 attention heads，而不是均匀分布在全网，这对架构设计、adapter 放置、蒸馏和稀疏干预都有意义。第二，图文对齐并不只是 encoder 侧表征问题，decoder/backbone 内部存在可定位、可操纵的时序路由机制。这个观察对理解多模态 token 如何进入统一 LM 很有价值，不过论文本身更偏 interpretability，贡献量级适合 skim，不属于 must-read 的 pretrain 方法论文。","technical_approach":"方法分三步。第一步是定位“阅读顺序/视觉叙述顺序”在网络中的层位置。作者用六格漫画作为受控环境，因为 panel 的空间顺序天然对应叙述顺序。对同一张 strip，分别给正常提示和“reverse”提示，让模型按相反顺序读取，并在回答前的残差流上做 difference-of-means，得到一个 per-layer 的 read-in-reverse direction。然后把这个方向注入新前向过程的某一层，观察答案是否从正常顺序翻到反向顺序。结果显示只有 20–28 层这一窄带有效，说明顺序控制主要局部化在中后层。\n\n第二步是在这些层里找具体的 gaze heads。作者定义了一个简单的相关性分数：看某个 head 在生成某段文本时，其 attention 是否稳定落在当前被描述的 panel 区域，并随叙述推进而切换。发现 Qwen3-VL-8B 的 1152 个头里，大约 100 个头满足这一性质，占 8.7%，集中在中后层。这个发现还在 2B 到 32B 的不同尺寸和其他 VLM 架构上复现，说明它不是单模型偶然现象。\n\n第三步是做因果干预。作者不训练额外模块，而是在推理时直接修改这些 gaze heads 的 attention mask，把它们重定向到指定 panel。结果模型会被迫去描述被指向的区域。关键点在于，这种干预有一个很窄的有效窗口：干预太少，控制力不够；干预太多，会破坏语言流畅性和整体生成，说明 gaze heads 负责“看哪里”，但不是全部生成机制。文中还展示了 mid-generation intervention：生成到一半再切换目标区域，模型会先收尾当前 panel，再转去描述新 panel，说明这些头参与的是在线的视觉-语言路由，而不是一次性全局规划。","experiments":"主实验基于 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct。模型是 36 层、每层 32 个头，共 1152 个 attention heads。发现阶段使用 COMICS 数据集中的 3948 个漫画样本，构造成六格横向拼接 strip；评估阶段使用 500 个由 GPT Image-1 生成、各 panel 视觉差异明确的六格漫画，避免 discovery data 泄漏。\n\n几个关键结果值得记。第一，read-in-reverse direction 的层定位实验表明，只有第 20–28 层注入该方向时，模型才会显著从左到右答案翻转为从右到左答案，说明顺序表征高度局部化。第二，作者识别出 top-100 gaze heads，少于总头数的 9%。第三，只对这些 gaze heads 做 attention 重定向，就能以 83.1% 的准确率把 VQA 答案引导到指定 panel，而随机非 gaze heads 的同类干预接近失败；文中给出的 chance 是 16.7%，对应六选一。第四，控制效果不是单调增加的：头数太少只能部分转向，头数太多会损伤生成，说明这些头是一个功能子回路，而不是越多越好。第五，这套发现不仅在漫画上成立，也能迁移到自然图像和其他 VLM 尺寸/架构上，支持其普遍性。","reading_guide":"泛读建议很明确。先看 Introduction 和 Experimental Setup，理解为什么作者选 comic strips 而不是自然图像：不是为了做漫画任务，而是为了把“叙述顺序”变成可观测、可验证的空间结构。然后重点看 Sec. 4/5 附近关于 read-in-reverse direction 和 gaze head discovery 的方法，以及对应图 1 和 head 分布图。最后看 redirection intervention 的结果图表，这部分最能说明相关性是否上升为因果性。\n\n如果只打算花 25–35 分钟，建议顺序是：层定位实验 → gaze head 打分方法 → top-100 干预结果 → 泛化到自然图像/其他模型。值得深挖的点有三个：一是这些 gaze heads 是否来自预训练阶段还是 instruction tuning 阶段形成；二是它们与已有 image heads / localization heads 的重叠程度；三是这种稀疏可控子回路能否反过来用于多模态训练时的 regularization、对齐诊断或 hallucination 抑制。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific internal question in VLMs: when a model describes an image, which part of the network tracks the visual region currently being verbalized, and does that mechanism actually control what gets described? Rather than identifying generic “image-attending heads,” the authors look for a temporally aligned subset of attention heads whose focus moves with the generated narration. They call these heads gaze heads and test whether they are merely correlated with grounding or causally sufficient to steer it.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining-method paper. It is closer to mechanistic interpretability for VLMs. Still, it has useful implications for multimodal pretraining. It suggests that visual grounding inside a language-model backbone may be concentrated in a relatively small mid-to-late-layer subcircuit rather than spread uniformly across the network. That matters for architecture design, adapter placement, sparse intervention, and possibly distillation. It also reinforces a broader point: image-text alignment is not only an encoder-side representation issue. There is an online routing mechanism inside the decoder/backbone that decides which visual tokens are being turned into language at each step.","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"$33","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and setup to understand why comics are used. The point is not comic understanding as a task, but a controlled spatial proxy for narration order. Then read the section on the reverse-reading direction and the layer-localization result, especially Figure 1. After that, focus on the gaze-head discovery criterion and the intervention results. Those are the parts that establish both localization and causality.\n\nA 25–35 minute pass is enough. stop_gradient(x_dense) 作为输入，只重构剩余部分。设计上有三个约束：第一，瓶颈保持线性，避免它学成另一个非线性稀疏编码器；第二，rank 很小，避免它抢走 SAE 应该解释的内容；第三，用 stop-gradient 做梯度隔离，减少瓶颈和 SAE 的协同投机。\n\n作者在 BatchTopK SAE 和 MatryoshkaBatchTopK SAE 上验证该结构。实验设置包括 Gemma-2-2B layer 12、字典大小 16,384、L0 或 k=40、瓶颈 rank=24。瓶颈参数量为 2*r*D，低于对应 SAE 参数量的 0.7%。这个设计的优点是改动小，不要求重写 SAE 架构；关键取舍是承认 residual stream 中有一部分内容不应强制追求稀疏单语义解释。","experiments":"主要结果集中在 Gemma-2-2B 第 12 层。加入 rank-24 线性瓶颈后，在 BatchTopK 和 Matryoshka 两种 SAE 架构上，dense latent 数量最多减少 84%，同时在 matched sparsity 下改善 SAEBench 中的 sparse probing 和 targeted probe perturbation 指标。\n\n因果干预结果更有信息量。移除瓶颈吸收的稠密成分会让 cross-entropy 升高 7.5x；相比之下，移除几何上接近的 top-24 PCA 方向只升高 2.8x。这个差异说明瓶颈学到的不是普通 PCA 子空间的简单替代，而是更贴近模型计算所需的方向。作者还显示，稀疏字典会冗余地编码这些内容：消融 787 个与该成分最对齐的稀疏 feature 只让 cross-entropy 升高 2.9x；消融 2,048 个 topic-aligned features 后，MMLU topic classification 几乎不变，而移除瓶颈成分会把该分类从 98.7% 降到接近随机水平。后验分析还把该成分与 top principal components 以及 Bondarenko outlier dimensions 联系起来。","reading_guide":"建议泛读时间 25 到 35 分钟。先看方法图和第 2 节，确认 rank-r bottleneck、stop-gradient 和 SAE residual input 的具体连接方式。然后看 Table 1，重点比较 dense latent count、SAEBench sparse probing、targeted probe perturbation 的变化。最值得细看的是因果消融部分：7.5x cross-entropy、787 个 aligned sparse features、2,048 个 topic-aligned features、98.7% 到 chance 这几组数字共同支撑了“计算 scaffold 不适合稀疏解释”的论点。附录 B 的 rank、linearity 和 gradient isolation 控制实验也建议扫一遍，因为这些控制决定了结论是否只是容量增加带来的副作用。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Standard sparse autoencoders are trained to reconstruct the entire residual stream, implicitly assuming that all activation content is suitable for sparse, approximately monosemantic decomposition. This work tests a different hypothesis: some residual-stream content is low-rank, dense, computationally important, and poorly matched to sparse dictionary coding. Forcing this content through an SAE may create persistent dense latents and consume dictionary capacity.","importance":"The paper is mainly relevant to mechanistic interpretability, but it has implications for how pretraining representations are analyzed. If residual streams contain dense computational scaffolds, then SAE features should not be treated as a complete decomposition of model state. Some directions may function more like global coordinates, outlier channels, or context/position infrastructure than semantic features. For researchers using SAEs to inspect pretrained models, the work provides a concrete warning: reconstruction quality and feature sparsity can be improved by separating dense low-rank structure before asking a sparse dictionary to explain the remainder.","technical_approach":"The method adds a rank-r linear bottleneck in parallel with a standard SAE. The bottleneck reconstructs a dense low-rank component, while the SAE is trained on the residual x - stop_gradient(x_dense). Three constraints are central. The bottleneck is linear, so it does not become another nonlinear feature learner. Its rank is small, so it cannot absorb all reconstructable structure and starve the SAE. Gradients are isolated with stop-gradient to reduce co-adaptation between the dense and sparse paths.\n\nThe design is tested with BatchTopK and MatryoshkaBatchTopK SAEs. The main setup uses Gemma-2-2B layer 12, a 16,384-feature dictionary, k or L0 equal to 40, and a rank-24 bottleneck. The added parameter count is 2*r*D, less than 0.7% of the SAE in this configuration. The method is deliberately minimal: it does not change the sparse autoencoder architecture or its basic training procedure, but changes what part of the activation the SAE is asked to explain.","experiments":"$34","reading_guide":"A 25-35 minute skim should cover the main contribution. Many monitoring, filtering, and steering methods assume that high-level concepts can be isolated by directions in activation space. Multilingual models are a hard case for that assumption because languages are correlated in structured ways. The paper separates two questions that are often conflated: whether language representations are similar, and whether language identity is causally separable enough for targeted intervention. For pretraining research, this suggests that multilingual evaluation should include representation coupling and cross-lingual spillover, not only downstream accuracy.","technical_approach":"The analysis follows a causal-geometric interpretability framework. A language is treated as a concept variable, and a bilingual contrast such as English-to-French is represented by activation differences between paired examples. If those differences are approximately parallel across examples, the contrast is considered to have a stable linear direction. The paper then uses a covariance-adjusted or causal inner product to rescale representation space so that independent concepts should become closer to orthogonal. This makes residual overlap more interpretable as structured dependence rather than an artifact of activation scale. The study covers 28 bilingual contrasts and 3 non-linguistic concepts across embedding/context and unembedding/logit-related spaces. Additive interventions along language directions are used as diagnostics for whether these directions produce predictable logit and generation effects.","experiments":"The experiments analyze Qwen3-4B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, and Llama-3-8B. The covariance-adjusted inner product reduces apparent overlap between language directions, making the remaining structure align better with linguistic similarity. Languages from the same family show simplex-like organization, suggesting that hierarchical language relations are reflected geometrically. Additive interventions produce systematic logit-level changes, but generation-level control is limited. This gap is important: a direction can be useful for diagnosis without being sufficient for reliable steering.","reading_guide":"A 15–25 minute skim should focus on the methodology for constructing bilingual contrast directions and defining the causal inner product. before generation, and whether self-repair induces a stable activation-space direction associated with repair success. The study uses the prompt-final hidden state of Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 on LiveCodeBench, rather than hidden states after code has been generated.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is in mechanistic diagnosis. The result suggests that externally verifiable code correctness has a strong pre-generation signal in the model representation. This is relevant to verifiers, adaptive test-time compute, early rejection, and possible process-level training signals derived from activations. The careful part is the confound analysis: the authors do not equate a high probe score with semantic knowledge. They use residualization to separate robust correctness information from simpler observable variables such as prompt length and repair-context covariates.","technical_approach":"The first analysis runs a prompt-only forward pass and extracts the hidden state at the final prompt token. A linear classifier is trained to predict whether the first generated solution will pass unit tests. The base model is frozen, upper transformer layers are considered, and the layer is chosen by nested cross-validation. To control for prompt length, each hidden dimension is residualized against length and the probe is retrained. The second analysis studies repair. For cases where the first attempt fails, the authors compute a per-task hidden-state delta from the failed attempt to the repair attempt, then compare deltas for successful and failed repairs. Before residualizing, they test whether candidate covariates differ across outcome groups. This matters because residualizing a balanced covariate can artificially erase a contrastive signal, while leaving an imbalanced covariate uncontrolled can create a spurious direction.","experiments":"The model is Qwen/Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, a 36-layer decoder-only transformer with 2560-dimensional hidden states. The dataset contains 444 LiveCodeBench tasks across easy, medium, and hard tiers. Hidden states are collected from layers 29–36, with the final layer choice made by nested CV. The pre-generation probe reaches a leakage-free held-out AUC of 0.931 ± 0.008 over 50 outer splits. After removing the linear effect of prompt length from every hidden dimension, the AUC remains 0.911 ± 0.010, well above a prompt-length-only baseline of 0.754 ± 0.014. For repair, 236 cleaned cases are analyzed. The raw repair delta direction passes magnitude and inclination tests against label-shuffled nulls, but the signal largely disappears after conditional residualization against three imbalanced repair-context covariates. Later repair attempts have sharply lower pass rates and too little data for the same direction analysis.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. JSONL+hash+ripgrep 这类强基线。作者承认 append-only log 可以覆盖部分能力，但认为 git 的差异在操作层：三方 merge、冲突显式暴露、签名作者、refs/tags、bundle 级别的 bit-identical 复现、pack-file 去重，以及现成工具链。实验上，作者把 substrate 与 accuracy 解耦：如果不同 substrate 在准确率上没有差异，那么选择 git 的理由主要来自可审计和可运维属性。","experiments":"核心实验比较五种记忆 substrate：none、markdown、vector、graph、git；覆盖 GPQA-Diamond 和 MATH-500，包含 cross-problem 与 cross-episode 两种迁移设置，使用两个 backbone，规模最高到 n=500。H-memory 的结论是否定的：没有任何记忆 substrate 在新问题上稳定提升准确率。一个 n=40 时 git 记忆约 +15 pp 的趋势没有通过预注册复现实验。相似度 sweep 给出更细的解释：当检索样本与测试问题接近近重复，cosine 相似度约大于 0.8 时，记忆带来明显收益，约 +12 到 +13.5 pp；低于这个阈值时基本没有收益。使用约 4.5 倍更强的 backbone 后，近重复收益变得更陡，约 +22.5 到 +28.5 pp，但“方法迁移”区域仍然接近零。真正稳定推动准确率的是 test-time sampling 和 base model 强度：self-consistency 在 n=500 时带来约 +3.4 pp。论文中 GPQA headline 结果 47.0% vs 33.0% 主要来自 MCQ-aware expansion 和更大的 compute budget，作者明确不把它归因于记忆。substrate 侧，git 写入开销约 15 ms/write，并保持与其他 substrate 的准确率 parity。另有一个 merge 功能演示：跨进程通过 central bare repository 合并，矛盾 lessons 会以 merge conflict 暴露，而简单拼接会静默保留冲突。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Introduction 中两个假设的定义，尤其是 H-substrate 和 H-memory 的分离；再看 Design 里 reasoning tree 到 git DAG 的映射表和图，这部分决定了方法边界。实验部分重点看 §4 的 substrate 对照和预注册复现实验，关注为什么早期 +15 pp 趋势被撤回；§5 的 similarity sweep 是全篇最关键结果，它把“记忆有效”限定到 copyability threshold；§6 用来理解 test-time sampling、MCQ-aware expansion 和 compute budget 的混杂因素。值得深挖的是作者如何控制 agent、backbone、检索 substrate 之外的变量，以及近重复收益如何区分 answer retrieval 与 method transfer。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a missing systems layer for agent reasoning: reasoning traces are usually transient, hard to replay, hard to audit, and hard to merge. It also tests a stronger empirical claim: whether cross-problem memory actually improves accuracy on novel tasks. The work separates these two questions. GitOfThoughts provides a version-controlled substrate for reasoning traces, while controlled experiments swap memory substrates under a fixed agent setup to measure whether memory itself helps.","importance":"The main value for foundation-model research is not a new pretraining method, but a careful negative result about test-time memory and reasoning reuse. Across novel problems, memory does not reliably improve accuracy; gains appear only when the retrieved case is close to a duplicate of the test item. That distinction matters for agent memory, synthetic reasoning reuse, continual training data construction, and retrieval-augmented reasoning. A system that claims to transfer past reasoning needs to show method transfer, not only answer retrieval from near-duplicate cases.","technical_approach":"GitOfThoughts represents an agent’s reasoning tree as a git repository. Each scored thought becomes a commit, scores are stored as notes, outcomes are represented with tags, and retrieval is implemented through git history search. The contribution is deliberately not a new tree-search algorithm. The outer search is a conventional Tree-of-Thoughts-style setup; the text states a depth-1 tree with branching factor 4, and for multiple-choice questions the four root children correspond to A/B/C/D. The inner execution loop uses ReAct-style plumbing. The paper also considers a strong engineering baseline: append-only JSONL with hashes and lexical search. The claimed advantage of git is operational rather than algorithmic: three-way merge with explicit conflict surfacing, signed authorship, native refs and tags, bit-identical reproduction through content addressing and bundles, pack-file deduplication, and mature tooling such as blame, bisect, hosting, and CI.","experiments":"$35","reading_guide":"Read the introduction for the separation between H-substrate and H-memory.
