The latter is the key result because it separates near-duplicate answer retrieval from method transfer. Section 6 is useful for understanding confounds from self-consistency, MCQ-aware expansion, and compute budget. The most useful details to inspect are the controls that keep the agent fixed while changing only the memory substrate.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"推翻Agent记忆有用共识的反常识工作，预注册实验设计和零结果对做长上下文、内置记忆或Agent的研究员是强信号，必须看§4的实验细节。","en":"Counterintuitive work falsifying the consensus that agent memory improves accuracy; pre-registered study design and null result are strong signals for long-context, built-in memory, or agent researchers, must read §4 experiment details."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Pavan C Shekar","Abhishek H S","Aswanth Krishnan"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["agent memory","version control","reasoning trace","pre-registered replication","null result"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14470","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.14470","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.14302","title":"Retrospective Progress-Aware Self-Refinement for LLM Agent Training","abstract":"LLM-based agents trained with reinforcement learning optimize step-wise action prediction but lack metacognitive awareness of task progress, inducing a gap that hinders long-horizon scaling. A pilot study reveals that online progress prompting hurts performance while retrospective demonstrations help, yet this capability cannot emerge from outcome-reward training alone. We present RePro, Retrospective Progress-Aware Training, a framework that trains agents to self-generate progress signals via a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-12T09:38:47Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2606_14302.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"长时程 agent 的 RL 训练通常只对“每一步选什么动作”给信号，但缺少对“任务进度/是否走在正确轨道上”的可学习表征与训练信号；直接在执行时强行让模型报进度（online progress prompting）反而会引入噪声、干扰决策，导致成功率下降。论文要解决的是：如何让 agent 在不依赖额外模块的情况下，学会可用的进度自评，并把它变成可用于长时程 credit assignment 的稠密训练信号。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关联不强（更偏 agent RL / post-train），但对“训练信号设计”和“从轨迹结果反推中间监督”的思路有启发：1）展示了一个清晰的训练动态现象——在线自评会伤性能，而基于完成轨迹的回顾式标注能提升；2）给出一种把稀疏 outcome reward 变成逐步 shaping 信号的构造方式，减少长时程任务的方差；3）对后续把类似的“回顾式自监督”迁移到 mid-train / continual pretrain（例如从长文档生成的最终质量反推段落级质量信号）有参考价值。整体贡献量级属于“训练 recipe + 信号工程”的中等增益，而非范式级改变。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 forward-then-reflect：同一条轨迹分两遍生成。\n- Forward：agent 正常与环境交互，在每一步除了动作外还输出一个在线进度估计（如 0-100%）。\n- Reflect（Retrospective reassessment）：episode 结束后，已知最终 outcome（成功/失败或得分），模型再回看整段轨迹，对每一步的进度重新评估，得到“回顾式进度”序列。关键假设是：进度在 hindsight 下更可辨别，能作为更可靠的中间监督。\n两阶段训练：\n1）Retrospection Warmup：用少量外部 LLM 生成的示范，教模型学会回顾式反思的输出格式与基本行为（相当于让模型先学会‘怎么写反思’）。\n2）RePro-PO：把回顾式进度用于强化学习的复合奖励（composite reward），包含三类项：\n  - retrospective progress shaping：用回顾式进度给每一步分配稠密奖励/惩罚，替代只在终点给 outcome reward 的高方差信号；\n  - online-retrospective alignment：约束在线进度与回顾式进度一致，避免在线自评继续成为噪声源；\n  - format regularization：约束输出满足进度格式/范围等，减少 reward hacking（例如乱报进度骗 shaping）。\n与常见做法的差异：不是额外训练一个 critic/价值函数去做 credit assignment，而是让 policy 自己产生一个可学习的“进度变量”，再用 hindsight 版本把它校准，并把它回灌成逐步奖励。","experiments":"论文先做 pilot study（WebShop，backbone 用 DeepSeek-V4 与 GPT-5.1）：\n- Online progress prompting 显著降成功率：DS-v4 35.9%→33.6%；GPT-5.1 44.1%→29.3%；平均约 -8.6%。随机进度与之接近（31.2%、32.4%），说明问题在于在线自评噪声干扰决策。\n- Retrospective demonstrations（用完成轨迹+结果做回顾式进度示范，测试时 ICL）提升成功率：DS-v4 35.9%→44.1（+8.2）；GPT-5.1 44.1%→51.6（+7.5）；平均 +7.9%。随机示范仅小幅提升（+2.0、+4.3）。\n主实验：在 WebShop / ALFWorld / Sokoban 上、不同模型规模下训练 RePro。WebShop 上相对 baseline RL 训练的绝对成功率提升分别为 +8.98、+11.57、+5.82（对应不同模型大小）。并报告消融均不如完整 RePro（具体消融项包括去掉 shaping / alignment / warmup 等）。\n此外做 progress quality 分析：RePro 的进度估计能更好地区分成功与失败轨迹（文中表述为“显著更好”，用于证明学到的是可用的 metacognition，而非格式化输出）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 25-35 分钟）：\n- 先看 Pilot Study 的实验设置与 Figure 1：这是全文最关键的现象证据，解释了为什么“在线 prompting”不等于“可学习能力”。\n- 再看方法部分对 composite reward 的三项分解：重点理解 shaping 如何从 hindsight 进度构造逐步信号，以及 alignment 如何抑制在线噪声。\n- 最后看 WebShop 的主结果表与消融：确认增益主要来自哪一项（shaping vs alignment vs warmup），以及是否存在对特定环境的过拟合迹象。\n如果只想抓住可迁移思想：把“回顾式可判别信号”蒸馏/对齐到“在线可用信号”，并用它做稠密训练。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"RL-trained LLM agents are typically optimized for step-wise action selection with sparse outcome rewards, but they lack a learnable notion of task progress (what is done, what remains, whether the trajectory is on track). Forcing progress estimation online via prompting introduces noisy self-assessments that disrupt decision-making. The paper targets a concrete question: how to make an agent learn usable progress self-assessment and turn it into dense training signals for long-horizon credit assignment, without adding extra modules.","importance":"This is more agent RL / post-training than pretraining, but it is relevant to pretraining researchers as a training-signal design case study. It (1) documents a sharp training-time asymmetry: online self-assessment hurts, hindsight-anchored progress helps; (2) provides a recipe to convert sparse terminal rewards into per-step shaping signals using retrospective labels; (3) suggests a general pattern that could transfer to mid-training or continual pretraining: derive intermediate supervision retrospectively from full sequences and align it to an online predictor. The contribution is a solid recipe-level improvement rather than a new modeling paradigm.","technical_approach":"$36","experiments":"Pilot study on WebShop with DeepSeek-V4 and GPT-5.1:\n- Online progress prompting reduces success: DS-v4 35.9%→33.6%; GPT-5.1 44.1%→29.3% (≈ -8.6% on average). 执行并与环境交互。\n- 受控组合：通过 typed composition 把“组件可替换”变成一等公民，允许在不重写整套 pipeline 的情况下替换某个模块（例如不同记忆表示、不同推理结构、是否加入反思），并观察性能与成本的变化。\n- 研究重点不是提出新的推理算法，而是把常见 agent 设计（含多轮 deliberation、记忆检索、反思、可选 RL）纳入同一设计空间，强调模块间的表示兼容性（memory 的粒度/格式是否匹配 reasoner 的决策方式）与闭环任务中的交互效应。","experiments":"在四个具身基准上做诊断式评测：DeliveryBench（长时程规划与资源/截止约束）、ALFRED（家务操作与任务状态跟踪）、MiniGrid（符号化导航与部分可观测）、RoboTHOR（真实感 3D embodied LLM agent “scaffolds” from tightly-coupled engineering pipelines into a controlled, composable design space with standardized interfaces, so that one can attribute gains to individual modules (memory/reasoning/reflection/action/optional learning) and study interaction effects in closed-loop embodied tasks.","importance":"Limited direct impact on foundation-model pretraining recipes, but useful for the pretrain↔post-train↔deployment interface: many capability gains come from inference-time scaffolding rather than model weights. Without a shared specification, it is hard to decide what should be learned during pretraining vs. attached at inference, and how to co-optimize them. The typed modularization can support future work on scaffold-aware mid-training, tool-use pretraining, and RL that trains policies to remain compatible with deployment-time scaffolds.","technical_approach":"AgentSpec defines an embodied agent as a typed composition over a Perception–Memory–Reasoning–Reflection–Action loop, with an optional Learning module.\n- Interface standardization: Perception maps raw observations to a canonical state; Memory retrieves history/knowledge in a fixed format; Reasoning proposes decisions; Reflection critiques/revises; Action executes in the environment.\n- Controlled recombination: modules become swappable components under typed interfaces, enabling ablations and comparisons without rebuilding the whole system.\n- The novelty is not a new reasoning algorithm, but a unifying specification that makes module boundaries explicit and highlights representation compatibility (e.g., memory granularity/format matching the reasoner) as a first-order factor in closed-loop performance.","experiments":"Evaluated on four embodied benchmarks with complementary stresses: DeliveryBench (long-horizon planning with deadlines/resources), ALFRED (compositional household manipulation with persistent state tracking), MiniGrid (symbolic navigation under partial observability), and RoboTHOR (realistic 3D navigation).\nKey findings: (1) module compatibility can matter as much as module strength; memory helps only when its representation aligns with the reasoning strategy, otherwise it distracts. (2) best compositions are environment-dependent; short/symbolic tasks lean on step-wise reasoning, long-horizon tasks are bottlenecked by state tracking and trajectory coherence. The former enables autonomous retrieval and integration of information in open-ended environments to tackle open-ended research tasks, yet it is constrained by the static parametric deep research capabilities of agent systems. The latter allows agents to autonomously interact with the environment to gain experiences that evolve model capabilities. However, it","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-10T15:03:42Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2606_13710.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的是开放式 deep research agent 的训练问题：这类任务没有标准答案，传统基于可验证答案的自博弈或 RL evolution 很难直接使用。","importance":"对基座预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 post-train 与持续能力增长有一定参考价值。论文关心的是如何在开放式任务中构造训练信号，而不是只在数学、代码这类可验证任务上做 RL。它的价值在于把 query proposer、tool-using solver 和动态 judge 放进同一个闭环，尝试维持任务难度、可学习性和奖励质量。这和预训练后的持续训练、合成数据生成、RL 信号设计都有联系。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution，简称 HOTE。系统包含三个共同演化的模块：solver 负责基于 ReAct 框架调用搜索工具、规划、整合信息并生成带引用的研究报告；judge 比较同一 query 下多个 solver 回答，动态生成 rubric，并用 rubric 给回答打奖励，避免依赖确定标准答案；proposer 根据 judge 发现的 solver 弱点进行信息搜索，并生成更有挑战但仍可学习的 query。训练上使用 GRPO 推动 proposer 和 solver 的博弈，同时让 judge 更新评价标准。论文还设计了 tool-use 与 no-tool 的双模式混合训练，使模型既学习检索环境中的长程研究流程，也保留无工具条件下的内部推理和写作能力。","experiments":"论文在三个 long-form deep research benchmark 上评估。主要结果是，经过 HOTE 训练的 8B 模型超过了若干开放 8B–32B deep research 模型和已有 deep research 训练方法，并且训练时间开销更低。消融结果强调三个模块都不可缺：只训练 solver、缺少 proposer 的难题生成、或缺少 judge 的动态 rubric，都会削弱效果。图示结果还显示，HOTE 能在训练中维持合成 query 的难度，并使 tool-use 和 no-tool 两种模式共同提升。给定材料中没有提供完整表格数字，细读时需要核对各 benchmark 的绝对分数、方差和 judge 设置。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 30–40 分钟。先看 Method 中三模块定义，尤其是 judge 如何从多回答比较中生成 rubric，以及 proposer 如何利用 solver 弱点生成 query。然后看 GRPO 目标和 hybrid training 细节，判断训练信号是否稳定、是否可能出现 reward hacking。实验部分重点看三类消融：去掉 proposer、去掉 judge、去掉 hybrid mode。还要关注 benchmark 的评测方式和 judge 可靠性，因为开放式 deep research 的结果很容易受评价协议影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how to train deep research agents on open-ended tasks where there are no deterministic gold answers, making standard self-play or RL evolution setups hard to apply.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to post-training, continual improvement, and synthetic task generation. The useful part is the training-signal design: the system tries to evolve tasks, solvers, and evaluators together, instead of relying on fixed datasets or verifiable-answer rewards. This is close to the problem of building scalable supervision for long-horizon agent behavior.","technical_approach":"HOTE consists of three interacting modules. The solver is a ReAct-style language model agent that plans, calls search tools, gathers evidence, and writes cited reports. The judge compares multiple solver outputs for the same query, constructs dynamic rubrics, and assigns rewards without requiring a single correct answer. The proposer uses the judge’s diagnosis of solver weaknesses, together with information seeking, to generate challenging but learnable research queries. GRPO is used to train the proposer–solver game. A dual-mode setup trains both tool-use and no-tool behavior, so the model learns external research workflows while also improving internal reasoning and report generation.","experiments":"The paper evaluates on three long-form deep research benchmarks. Ablations indicate that the proposer, solver, and judge all contribute; removing any part weakens the training loop. The paper also reports that HOTE maintains query difficulty during training and improves both tool-use and no-tool modes. This finding has direct implications for how ablation studies and result reporting should be conducted for such systems.","technical_approach":"On the ogbn-arxiv text-attributed graph node classification task, a pretrained and frozen GCN is exposed to a ReAct-style LLM agent as an explicit tool (returning predicted label, anomaly score, link probabilities). Four arms are compared: Agent+GNN-tool, Agent+minimal graph-navigation tool, GNN-only, and agent-only with no graph tool. Cross-scale analysis: from 0.5B to 7B, once a model can use the tool at all (≥1.5B), agreement with GNN grows from 60% to 98%, with stronger models showing more deference. Oracle gap at 7B is 0.12/0.18/0.22 (low/mid/high homophily), roughly doubling at high homophily compared to 3B, because the neighbor-label tool overtakes the GNN at high homophily yet the agent still defers. A simple selective-invocation gate recovers about half the gap where informative but yields no net global gain, and a learned four-feature router does no better. Information ceiling analysis shows standard test-time uncertainty features can recover at most 1/6 to 1/3 of the oracle headroom. This increases the chance of observing at least one success on hard cases and exposes unstable predictions on easy ones. Second, Self-Verified Cross-View Anchoring adds an oracle coordinate only after the current policy has already produced a max-reward rollout in that group.
